问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：网络 VCC3V3 上连接的主要芯片包括：**U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804**。 **支撑证据**： 证据 [1] 显示，网络 VCC3V3 直接连接到以下芯片的对应引脚： - U1500.VIN - U1600.VCCIN - U1700.VIN_B0_1、U1700.VIN_B1_1、U1700.VANA、U1700.VIN_B0_2、U1700.VIN_B1_2 - U1800.VIN、U1800.VIN、U1800.MODE/SYNC - U1802.VIN - U1804.VIN、U1804.VIN 其他元件（电容、电阻、电感、测试点、晶体管 Q1700、连接器 X2000）虽也连接到该网络，但非主要集成电路，故不列入。
评分正文：网络 VCC3V3 上连接的主要芯片包括：**U1500、U1600、U1700、U1800、U1802、U1804**。 **支撑证据**： 证据 [1] 显示，网络 VCC3V3 直接连接到以下芯片的对应引脚： - U1500.VIN - U1600.VCCIN - U1700.VIN_B0_1、U1700.VIN_B1_1、U1700.VANA、U1700.VIN_B0_2、U1700.VIN_B1_2 - U1800.VIN、U1800.VIN、U1800.MODE/SYNC - U1802.VIN - U1804.VIN、U1804.VIN 其他元件（电容、电阻、电感、测试点、晶体管 Q1700、连接器 X2000）虽也连接到该网络，但非主要集成电路，故不列入。

hw-v1-joint-main-pmicsuccess0.6230.8270.750not_applicable0.3330.0000.5000.429not_applicable
问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：**主要结论** - **主 PMIC 型号**：LP87702‑Q1（具体型号 LP87702RRHBRQ1）。 - **输出电压轨**：VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50（均为从 VCC3V3 转换得到）。 - **软件配置方法**： - 通过 MCU 的 I2C0 总线（SCL = P15.4，SDA = P15.5，设备地址 0x08）设置 VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 的输出电压及使能。 - 通过 MCU 的 GPIO P15.3 使能 VCC50（5V 输出）。 - MCU 通过 GPIO P12.1 接收 LP87702 的中断信号（nINT），用于状态监控。 - 看门狗配置由 LP87702 的 OTP 文件固化（长周期 600ms，窗口 10‑30ms，复位低电平持续 10ms），非软件动态配置。 **支撑证据**（最少必需） 1. **型号确认**：电路图中实例 U1700 标记为 LP87702‑Q1（LP87702RRHBRQ1）[证据 2]。 2. **电压轨**：文档描述 LP87702 将 VCC3V3 转换为 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，分别用于 EQ6/CAM/DDR/FLASH、EQ6/DDR、CAN 收发器供电 [证据 10, 17]。 3. **软件配置**： - I2C 通信控制：使用 MCU_SCL_P15.4 和 MCU_SDA_P15.5，地址 0x08，设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 输出电压及使能 [证据 10, 17, 19]。 - VCC50 使能：由 MCU 的 P15.3 引脚控制 [证据 10, 17, 18]。 - 中断监控：LP87702 的 nINT 引脚连接 MCU 的 P12.1，用于中断信号 [证据 1, 19]。 - 看门狗 OTP 配置：窗口参数由 LP87702 的 OTP 文件定义，非软件运行时配置 [证据 13]。 **冲突与缺失** - **冲突**：关于 MCU 引脚 P12.1 的功能，文档[17]声称“用 MCU 的 P12.1 引脚复位该芯片”，但电路及引脚定义表[19]明确该引脚为 LP87702 的 **nINT 输出**（中断信号），且 MCU 方向为输入。两者矛盾，无法确认复位功能，应以电路定义为准（中断输入）。 - **缺失**：LP87702 运行期间需要通过 I2C 配置的具体寄存器列表（Table 5‑2）在证据中未提供，因此无法给出寄存器地址与数值的详细配置。
评分正文：**主要结论** - **主 PMIC 型号**：LP87702‑Q1（具体型号 LP87702RRHBRQ1）。 - **输出电压轨**：VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50（均为从 VCC3V3 转换得到）。 - **软件配置方法**： - 通过 MCU 的 I2C0 总线（SCL = P15.4，SDA = P15.5，设备地址 0x08）设置 VCC1V8 和 VCC1V1 的输出电压及使能。 - 通过 MCU 的 GPIO P15.3 使能 VCC50（5V 输出）。 - MCU 通过 GPIO P12.1 接收 LP87702 的中断信号（nINT），用于状态监控。 - 看门狗配置由 LP87702 的 OTP 文件固化（长周期 600ms，窗口 10‑30ms，复位低电平持续 10ms），非软件动态配置。 **支撑证据**（最少必需） 1. **型号确认**：电路图中实例 U1700 标记为 LP87702‑Q1（LP87702RRHBRQ1）[证据 2]。 2. **电压轨**：文档描述 LP87702 将 VCC3V3 转换为 VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50，分别用于 EQ6/CAM/DDR/FLASH、EQ6/DDR、CAN 收发器供电 [证据 10, 17]。 3. **软件配置**： - I2C 通信控制：使用 MCU_SCL_P15.4 和 MCU_SDA_P15.5，地址 0x08，设置 1.8V 和 1.1V 输出电压及使能 [证据 10, 17, 19]。 - VCC50 使能：由 MCU 的 P15.3 引脚控制 [证据 10, 17, 18]。 - 中断监控：LP87702 的 nINT 引脚连接 MCU 的 P12.1，用于中断信号 [证据 1, 19]。 - 看门狗 OTP 配置：窗口参数由 LP87702 的 OTP 文件定义，非软件运行时配置 [证据 13]。 **冲突与缺失** - **冲突**：关于 MCU 引脚 P12.1 的功能，文档[17]声称“用 MCU 的 P12.1 引脚复位该芯片”，但电路及引脚定义表[19]明确该引脚为 LP87702 的 **nINT 输出**（中断信号），且 MCU 方向为输入。两者矛盾，无法确认复位功能，应以电路定义为准（中断输入）。 - **缺失**：LP87702 运行期间需要通过 I2C 配置的具体寄存器列表（Table 5‑2）在证据中未提供，因此无法给出寄存器地址与数值的详细配置。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. MDIO is accessible and Link is in a connected state. Reset mode UV or Reset event, the PHY chip will Reset, during reset will not be able to LINKUPMDIO cannot bus access. Standby mode The device will automatically enter standby mode upon power-up only if*the PHY is configured in Master mode and set to managed mode. If the PHY operates in auto mode, it will automatically enter normal mode after power-on. 4. Sleep mode The PHY enters sleep mode when a sleep command is issued and the MDI bus is idle. In this mode, all PHY modules are disabled except for energy detection functionality. Note that all register configurations will be lost during sleep mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F26</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求MCU外置看门狗，用于监控MCU功能异常时进行复位。 The system requires MCU external watchdog, used to monitor MCU function abnormal reset.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>窗口看门狗喂狗参数由LP87702的OTP文件进行配置，具体参数为：喂狗长周期600ms，窗口为10~30ms，看门狗输出复位信号低电平持续10ms。用MCU的P13.3引脚喂狗。 The window watchdog feeding parameters are configured by the OTP file of LP87702, the specific parameters are: feeding the dog for a long period of 600ms, the window is 10~30ms, and the low level of the watchdog output reset signal lasts for 10ms. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. 问题：VCC0V75 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：**VCC0V75电源由TPS62872芯片（实例U1800）产生，输入电源为VCC3V3。** - **产生芯片**：TPS62872 (实例U1800)。 - 文档[10]（F14模块方案）明确指出：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872”。 - 电路设计[4]中U1800的型号标注为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1。 - 电路网络[3]显示VCC0V75连接到U1800的VOSNS引脚。 - **输入电源**：VCC3V3。 - 文档[10]（F11-F27模块方案）中“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B))”段落描述：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电”。 - 文档[14]（F14模块方案）同样说明：“采用TPS62872电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75”。 - **文档中的配置要求**： 1. **使能控制**：通过MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片（网络名MCU_VCC0V75_EN连接U900E.P22.0和U1804.EN），证据[1][10][14]。 2. **输出设置**：通过I2C通信（引脚MCU_SDA_P15.5、MCU_SCL_P15.4）设置输出电压和进行通信，证据[10][14]。 3. **电压精度**：ADC采样精度要求为0.75V±3.3%（或±5%），硬件滤波截止频率≥121.7Hz，滤波时间≤1.6ms，证据[8]（0V75采样表）。 4. **软件监控阈值**：软件应设过欠压阈值为0.673V（Min）~0.836V（Max），证据[11]（电源轨阈值表）。 **缺失信息**：无。所有子问题均有直接证据覆盖。
评分正文：**VCC0V75电源由TPS62872芯片（实例U1800）产生，输入电源为VCC3V3。** - **产生芯片**：TPS62872 (实例U1800)。 - 文档[10]（F14模块方案）明确指出：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872”。 - 电路设计[4]中U1800的型号标注为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1。 - 电路网络[3]显示VCC0V75连接到U1800的VOSNS引脚。 - **输入电源**：VCC3V3。 - 文档[10]（F11-F27模块方案）中“VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B))”段落描述：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电”。 - 文档[14]（F14模块方案）同样说明：“采用TPS62872电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为VCC0V75”。 - **文档中的配置要求**： 1. **使能控制**：通过MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片（网络名MCU_VCC0V75_EN连接U900E.P22.0和U1804.EN），证据[1][10][14]。 2. **输出设置**：通过I2C通信（引脚MCU_SDA_P15.5、MCU_SCL_P15.4）设置输出电压和进行通信，证据[10][14]。 3. **电压精度**：ADC采样精度要求为0.75V±3.3%（或±5%），硬件滤波截止频率≥121.7Hz，滤波时间≤1.6ms，证据[8]（0V75采样表）。 4. **软件监控阈值**：软件应设过欠压阈值为0.673V（Min）~0.836V（Max），证据[11]（电源轨阈值表）。 **缺失信息**：无。所有子问题均有直接证据覆盖。

None：HI-Z; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F08/F09</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统需求需要板内电源电压进行ADC采样和异常监控，要求功能安全达到ASIL-B等级，确保EQ6系统工作安全。 The system requirements require the on-board power supply voltage for ADC sampling and anomaly monitoring, and the functional safety is required to reach ASIL-B level to ensure the working safety of EQ6 system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>EQ6系统电源电压采样 系统需求需要MCU对LP87702、TPS62872、SA23002、TPS22918和摄像头端的电压VCC3V3_EQ，VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC3V3_CAM，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM进行ADC采样和异常监控。其中VCC1V8，VCC1V1，VCC0V75，0V75_MIPI_DDR，VCC_1V8_CAM、VCC1V1_CAM电压范围未超量程对电压直接采样。VCC3V3_EQ和VCC3V3_CAM经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_EQ用AN2引脚采样，VCC1V8用AN3引脚采样，VCC1V1用AN4引脚采样，VCC1V8_EQ用AN5引脚采样，VCC3V3_CAM用AN12引脚采样，VCC1V1_CAM用AN13引脚采样，VCC1V8_CAM用AN14引脚采样，VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR用AN18引脚采样，VCC0V75用AN19引脚采样。 EQ6 system power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the voltages VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC3V3_CAM, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM for the LP87702, TPS62872, SA23002, TPS22918, and camera-side devices. Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . Then, when soft start is complete, the device ramps up or down to the new value. The device can start up into a prebiased output. In this case, only a portion of the internal voltage ramp is seen externally (see Figure 9-9). Note that the device always operates in DcM during the start-up ramp, regardless of other configuration settings or operating conditions. 9.3.5SwitchingFrequencySelection During device initialization, a resistor-to-digital converter in the device determines the state of the FSEL pin and sets the switching frequency of the DC/DC converter according to Table 9-2. For a reliable voltage setting, ensure there is no stray current path connected to the FSEL pin and that the1. parasitic capacitance between the FSEL pin and GND is less than 1o0 pF. identical circuit detects the state of the VSEL pin - see Section 9.3.6.2). Detection of the state of the FSEL pin works as follows: To detect the most significant bit (MSB), the circuit opens S1 and S2, and the input buffer detects if a high or a low level is connected to the FSEL pin. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 To detect the least significant bit (LSB): If the MSB was 0, the circuit closes S1. If the input buffer detects a high level, LSB = 1. If the circuit detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the MSB was 1, the circuit closes S2. If the input buffer detects a low level, LSB = 0. If the circuit detects a· | high level, LSB = 1. 9.3.6 Output Voltage Setting 9.3.6.1 Output Voltage Range The device has four different voltage ranges. The VRANGE[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register control which range is active (see Table 9-3). The default output voltage range after device initialization is 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps. Note that every change to the VRANGE[1:0] bits must be followed by a write to the VSET register, even if the value of the VSET[7:o] bits does not change. This sequence is required for the device to start to use the new voltage range. Also note that the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range uses a 0.8-V reference and the other ranges use a 0.4-V reference. Switching to and from the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range can therefore cause increased output voltage undershoot or overshoot while the device switches its internal reference. In device variants that do not have I?C, the output voltage range is factory-set to 0.4 V to 1.675 V. 9.3.6.2OutputVoltageSetpoint Together with the selected range, the VSET[7:0] bits in the VSET register control the output voltage setpoint of the device (see Table 9-4). During initialization, the device reads the state of the VSEL pin and selects the default output voltage according to Table 9-5. Note that the VSEL pin also selects the I?C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). If the user programs new output voltage setpoint (VOUT[7:0]), output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]), or soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings when the device has already begun its soft-start sequence, the device ignores the new values until the soft-start sequence is complete. If the user changes the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, ramps up or down to the new value. If the user changes VOUT[7:0], VRAMP[1:0], or SSTIME[1:0] while EN is low, the device uses the new values the next time the user enables it. | TPS6287x-Q1 2.7-V to 6-V Input, 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, 15-A, Stackable, Synchronous Step- Down Converters with Fast Transient Response 3 Description 1 Features AEC-Q100 qualified for automotive applications The TPS6287x-Q1 is a family of pin-to-pin 6-A, 9- Temperature grade 1: -40°C to +125°C, TA A, 12-A, and 15-A synchronous step-down DC/DC converters with differential remote sensing. For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F13</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电。VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电。VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。 VCCIV8 is used to power EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH.VCC1V1 is used to power EQ6,DDR. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><caption> Table9-5.DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoints</caption> <tr><td >VSEL Pin(1)</td><td >Device Number</td><td >VSET[7:0]</td><td >Output Voltage Setpoint</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287x-Q1</td><td >0x5a</td><td >850 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY0-Q1</td><td >0x50</td><td >800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY1-Q1</td><td >0x14</td><td >500 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY2-Q1</td><td >0x64</td><td >1800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td >6.2 kQ to GND</td><td >TPS6287xY3-Q1</td><td >0x52</td><td >810 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN0-Q1</td><td >0x14</td><td >500 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN1-Q1</td><td >0x50</td><td >800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN2-Q1</td><td >0x64</td><td >1800 mV</td></tr> </table>

问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节说明 VCC3V3_MCU 存在 3V3 过压保护电路：当 VCC3V3 超过 4.03V 时，比较器输出关闭 NMOS 与 PMOS，切断 VCC3V3_SW；EDF 可补充网络与二极管或 TVS 拓扑，但不能据此断言未被文档直接描述的保护能力。
实际回答：**直接结论：** 3.3V电源（具体为VCC3V3_MCU）设计有过压保护，通过一个由基准源、NMOS和PMOS组成的电路实现。 **支撑证据：** 1. **存在过压保护电路：** * 硬件设计方案中明确包含“3V3过压保护电路”模块（F18），其功能是“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”。[证据4， 证据5] * 该模块的设计依据是“AOU要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对3V3_MCU进行电压监控”。[证据8] 2. **保护机制实现方式：** * 该保护电路的工作原理基于比较器或基准源控制MOSFET开关。[证据8] * **正常工作状态：** 当VCC3V3电压低于4.03V但高于开关管门限电压时，NMOS和PMOS导通，VCC3V3正常供电。[证据8] * **过压保护状态：** 当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，保护电路动作，NMOS和PMOS关断，VCC3V3_SW无电，从而断开后级负载，实现过压保护。[证据8] **缺失信息/界限：** * 证据明确指出该过压保护电路是针对 **VCC3V3_MCU** 这一特定电源轨。对于系统内其他由VCC3V3转换而来的电源轨（如VCC3V3_EQ、VCC3V3_ETH、VCC1V8等），本检索证据中未发现类似的独立硬件过压保护电路描述。 * 证据描述了保护电路的功能和触发点（4.03V），但未给出电路中具体器件的型号（如基准源型号、MOSFET型号），也未提供电路原理图的具体连接细节。
评分正文：**直接结论：** 3.3V电源（具体为VCC3V3_MCU）设计有过压保护，通过一个由基准源、NMOS和PMOS组成的电路实现。 **支撑证据：** 1. **存在过压保护电路：** * 硬件设计方案中明确包含“3V3过压保护电路”模块（F18），其功能是“用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护”。[证据4， 证据5] * 该模块的设计依据是“AOU要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对3V3_MCU进行电压监控”。[证据8] 2. **保护机制实现方式：** * 该保护电路的工作原理基于比较器或基准源控制MOSFET开关。[证据8] * **正常工作状态：** 当VCC3V3电压低于4.03V但高于开关管门限电压时，NMOS和PMOS导通，VCC3V3正常供电。[证据8] * **过压保护状态：** 当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，保护电路动作，NMOS和PMOS关断，VCC3V3_SW无电，从而断开后级负载，实现过压保护。[证据8] **缺失信息/界限：** * 证据明确指出该过压保护电路是针对 **VCC3V3_MCU** 这一特定电源轨。对于系统内其他由VCC3V3转换而来的电源轨（如VCC3V3_EQ、VCC3V3_ETH、VCC1V8等），本检索证据中未发现类似的独立硬件过压保护电路描述。 * 证据描述了保护电路的功能和触发点（4.03V），但未给出电路中具体器件的型号（如基准源型号、MOSFET型号），也未提供电路原理图的具体连接细节。

this topology does not confirm short-circuit protection capability. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM 问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：板上共使用了3个TPS22918电源开关，分别为U1500、U1802、U1803。每个开关的输入（VIN）、输出（VOUT）和使能（ON）连接如下： - **U1500**：输入 VCC3V3，输出 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN - **U1802**：输入 VCC3V3，输出 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN - **U1803**：输入 VCC1V8，输出 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN **支撑证据**： 证据[1]、[2]、[3]提供了U1500、U1802、U1803的完整引脚映射，包含VIN、VOUT、ON网络连接；证据[5]、[6]、[7]确认三个实例均为TPS22918器件。所有证据均来自同一原理图源文件825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF。 **缺失信息**：无。
评分正文：板上共使用了3个TPS22918电源开关，分别为U1500、U1802、U1803。每个开关的输入（VIN）、输出（VOUT）和使能（ON）连接如下： - **U1500**：输入 VCC3V3，输出 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN - **U1802**：输入 VCC3V3，输出 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN - **U1803**：输入 VCC1V8，输出 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN **支撑证据**： 证据[1]、[2]、[3]提供了U1500、U1802、U1803的完整引脚映射，包含VIN、VOUT、ON网络连接；证据[5]、[6]、[7]确认三个实例均为TPS22918器件。所有证据均来自同一原理图源文件825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF。 **缺失信息**：无。

问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据现有证据，以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源轨节点名称及其上游电源转换器件和级联关系**均无法确认**。具体如下： --- ### 1. 以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源轨节点名称或标号 **直接结论**：从证据中可看到 PHY（U1400）的电源引脚分配，但**未明确标注哪个引脚或网络对应 1.0V 电源轨**。 **支撑证据**： - U1400 的引脚映射中，电源相关引脚为： - VDDA → 网络 N22956977 - VDDIO / VDDMAC → 网络 N22956687 - VSLEEP → 网络 VSLEEP （来源：证据[1] `circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1400`） 这些网络的电压值在给定证据中未给出。虽然原理图中存在网络 `&1V0_ETH_ACQ`（证据[6]），但它连接的是 MCU 的 ADC 采样通道（U900A），而非 PHY 电源引脚。因此，**无法确定 PHY 的 1.0V 电源轨节点名称**。 --- ### 2. 上游电源转换器件及级联关系 **直接结论**：**缺失**——当前检索未找到该 1.0V 电源轨上游的任何电源转换器件（如 LDO、DC-DC）或其级联关系。 **缺失说明**：所有检索工具（电路查询、文档检索、表格检索）均未返回相关证据。无任何器件或连接描述说明该电源轨的生成路径。 （来源：检索账本中 sq_2 状态为 `missing`，缺失证据类型包括 `document_text`、`spreadsheet_table`、`circuit_design`）

ID</td><td>F01/F07/F25/F28/F31</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS产品支持KL15唤醒、CAN唤醒（平台预留）和以太网唤醒（平台预留）三种唤醒方式，当唤醒信号有效时直接使能一级电源，MCU开始工作并启动ADAS产品，并且MCU可以通过MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0信号辅助使能一级电源，实现off delay功能。 ADAS products support KL15 wake up, CAN wake up（Platform reservation），ETH wake up（Platform reservation）, three wake up modes. When the wake up signal is effective, the first-level power supply will be directly enabled. MCU starts to work and start the ADAS product, and the MCU can enable the first-level power supply through the MCU_WAKE_HOLD_P12.0 signal assist to achieve the off delay function.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL15唤醒 KL15 Wake Up KL15分成两路信号： The KL15 signal divides into two signals: 一路经过防反二极管和分压电阻后用于使能一级电源LN10046，当KL15为逻辑高时有效，一级电源开始工作。并且MCU得电启动后会使能MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚，通过防反接二极管辅助使能一级电源并在唤醒源掉电后实现OFF DELAY功能，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 One way passes through an anti-reverse diode and two divider resistors , used to enable the EN pin of the primary power supply LN10046, effective when the KL15 is logically high, the primary power supply starts working. The MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin will be enabled after the MCU is powered on, and the primary power supply will be enabled through the anti-reverse diode assist, and the function of OFF DELAY will be performed after the wake source is powered off to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks. 另一路通过防反接二极管和100K,1%、10K,1%分压电路，用于MCU的ADC采样进行是否有效判断。 用到的引脚网络是WKUP_ACQ，对应MCU的AN0引脚。 The other way through the anti-reverse diode and 100K,1%, 20K,1% voltage divider circuit, used for MCU ADC sampling to determine whether effective. The pin network used is WKUP_ACQ, which corresponds to the AN0 pin of the MCU. CAN特定帧唤醒 ADAS平台支持特定报文的CAN唤醒功能，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。 CAN通讯接口，TCAN1145配置时支持CAN特定报文唤醒，TCAN1145的VBAT引脚接至UB_PR引脚，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当CAN总线接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。INH信号通过一个防范二极管在通过100K和10K电阻分压后用MCU的AN11引脚采样。 2.CAN0 bus wake up The ADAS platform reserves the CAN wake-up function to support specific messages. When the wake-up signal is effective, the enable signal of the first-level power supply will be directly enabled, the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the entire ADAS product. CAN communication interface, TCAN1145 supports CAN specific message wake-up when configured, the VBAT pin of TCAN1145 is connected to UB_PR pin, The INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the CAN bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, which will enable the primary power and the product will start to work. The INH signal is sampled with the AN11 pin of the MCU via a guard diode after voltage splitting through 100K and 33K resistors. 3.ETH报文唤醒 ADAS产品唤醒支持ETH报文唤醒，当唤醒信号有效时会直接使能一级电源的使能信号，系统开始唤醒，MCU工作并启动整个ADAS产品工作。ETH PHY DP83TC812R的VSLEEP引脚接至常电，INH引脚通过分压和防反电路接至一级电源使能引脚，当以太网接收到唤醒报文时INH引脚为逻辑高，会使能一级电源，产品开始工作。 3.Flexray specific packet wakeup The wakeup of ADAS products supports the wakeup of ETH message. When the wakeup signal is effective, the enable signal of the primary power supply will be directly enabled, and the system will start to wake up, and the MCU will work and start the whole ADAS product. The VSLEEP pin of the PHY DP83TC812R is connected to the Constant Power Supply, and the INH pin is connected to the primary power enable pin through the voltage divider and anti-reverse circuit. When the ETH bus receives the wake-up message, the INH pin is logically high, and the primary power is enabled and the product starts to work. 4.自保持唤醒 MCU_WKUP_HOLD 当CAN总线唤醒报文中断后，INH引脚电平会无效（逻辑低），此时MCU会通过MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0引脚进入OFF DELAY功能自保持唤醒一段时间，确保MCU完成所有必要任务之后ADAS产品进入休眠。 4. Self keeping wake up When the CAN bus wake-up frame is interrupted, the INH pin level will be invalid (low logic), and the MCU will enter the OFF DELAY function through the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 pin to keep awake for a period of time to ensure that the ADAS product will enter hibernation after the MCU completes all necessary tasks.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F16</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关TPS22918进行设计。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3-EQ is used for the IO and camera power supply of EQ6, and the load switch TPS22918 is used for the design.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC3V3系统电源转换为VCC3V3_EQ，提供给EQ6和摄像头供电。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 The TPS22918 load switch is used to convert the VCC3V3 system power to VCC3V3-EQ to supply power to EQ6 and the camera. 问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：主控MCU的型号存在证据冲突，两个不同来源给出了互斥的取值，无法合并为一个确定结论。 **证据冲突列表**（字段：main_mcu.model）： | 取值 | 来源 | |------|------| | `tc367`（具体型号：SAK-TC367DP-64F300S） | 电路原理图文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` | | `tc377`（具体型号：SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA） | 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` | **支撑证据说明**： 1. **tc367 取值依据**： - 原理图 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中，实例 `U900A`～`U900E` 的器件编号均为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`（证据[1]～[5]）。 - 该原理图中的网络名称包含 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI` 等（证据[9]～[13]），直接关联到 MCU 的 JTAG 信号，进一步指向 TC367 系列器件。 2. **tc377 取值依据**： - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中“模块方案”部分明确写道：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”（证据[16]）。 **缺失信息**： - 两个来源均描述的是同一套ADAS系统中的“主控 MCU”，但给出的型号分属不同系列（TC367 vs TC377）。目前没有证据解释两者之间的关系（例如是否为不同功能模块、不同硬件版本、物料替代方案或文档描述错误）。需要额外的版本对比或设计说明才能确认正确取值。
评分正文：主控MCU的型号存在证据冲突，两个不同来源给出了互斥的取值，无法合并为一个确定结论。 **证据冲突列表**（字段：main_mcu.model）： | 取值 | 来源 | |------|------| | `tc367`（具体型号：SAK-TC367DP-64F300S） | 电路原理图文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` | | `tc377`（具体型号：SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA） | 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` | **支撑证据说明**： 1. **tc367 取值依据**： - 原理图 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF` 中，实例 `U900A`～`U900E` 的器件编号均为 `SAK-TC367DP-64F300S`（证据[1]～[5]）。 - 该原理图中的网络名称包含 `TC367_JTAG_TCK/DAP0`、`TC367_JTAG_TDI` 等（证据[9]～[13]），直接关联到 MCU 的 JTAG 信号，进一步指向 TC367 系列器件。 2. **tc377 取值依据**： - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中“模块方案”部分明确写道：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片”（证据[16]）。 **缺失信息**： - 两个来源均描述的是同一套ADAS系统中的“主控 MCU”，但给出的型号分属不同系列（TC367 vs TC377）。目前没有证据解释两者之间的关系（例如是否为不同功能模块、不同硬件版本、物料替代方案或文档描述错误）。需要额外的版本对比或设计说明才能确认正确取值。

clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. (continued) The switching frequency is resistor-selectable through the FSEL pin. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. The user cannot select the start-up voltage and I2C address independently.(1) The user can use the VSEL pin to select which of the four start-up voltages the device uses. For more information, see Table 9-5 and (2) 7 Pin Configuration and Functions 8 Specifications 8.1 Absolute Maximum Ratings over operating temperature range (unless otherwise noted)(1) (1)Operation outside the Absolute Maximum Ratings may cause permanent device damage. Absolute Maximum Ratings do not imply functional operation of the device at these or any other conditions beyond those listed under Recommended Operating Conditions. If used outside the Recommended Operating Conditions but within the Absolute Maximum Ratings, the device may not be fully functional, and this may affect device reliability, functionality, performance, and shorten the device lifetime. (2)All voltage values are with respect to the GND pin. While switching. 问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：根据检索证据，U1800的输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络总结如下： - **输入信号**：U1800的电源输入引脚VIN连接到VCC3V3网络。 - **输出检测**：U1800的输出检测通过VOSNS和GOSNS引脚实现。其中VOSNS连接到VCC0V75网络，GOSNS连接到GND。 - **使能控制**：U1800通过EN引脚进行硬件使能控制，EN引脚连接到N19888813网络。 - **I²C控制网络**：U1800的I²C控制通过SDA和SCL引脚连接，SDA连接到MCU_SDA_P15.5网络，SCL连接到MCU_SCL_P15.4网络。其I²C目标地址由VSEL引脚在上电时的状态决定，根据器件型号TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1，其默认I2C地址为0x42。 ### 详细依据 #### 输入信号 - **证据来源**：`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`，来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 - **证据内容**：该引脚映射显示，U1800的VIN引脚网络名为VCC3V3，这表明VCC3V3是U1800的电源输入。其他输入相关引脚如EN、MODE/SYNC、VSEL、FSEL的网络连接也一并列出，但它们属于控制或配置功能，并非主电源输入。 #### 输出检测 - **证据来源**：`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`，来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 - **证据内容**：该引脚映射显示，U1800的输出电压检测正端VOSNS连接到VCC0V75网络，检测负端GOSNS连接到GND。补偿管脚COMP连接至N19809013网络。 #### 使能控制 - **证据来源**：`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`，来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 - **证据内容**：该引脚映射显示，U1800的使能引脚EN连接至N19888813网络，表明U1800通过此网络上的信号进行硬件使能控制。 #### I²C控制网络 - **证据来源**：[1] `circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800` (来自 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`)，[2] `document:4e4a4edc9a902323` (来自 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 的 Table 6-1)。 - **证据内容**： - 电路设计证据（来源1）表明，U1800的I²C数据线SDA连接到MCU_SDA_P15.5，时钟线SCL连接到MCU_SCL_P15.4。 - 器件手册证据（来源2）中的Table 6-1列出，对于型号为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1的器件，其I²C地址为0x42。该表格的注释指出，I²C地址与启动电压相关，而启动电压由VSEL引脚的选择决定。结合电路设计中VSEL连接到N19808994，表明地址是由硬件配置的。 ### 缺失信息 根据现有证据，所有子问题的信息均已覆盖，未有明确缺失项。
评分正文：根据检索证据，U1800的输入、输出检测、使能和I²C控制网络总结如下： - **输入信号**：U1800的电源输入引脚VIN连接到VCC3V3网络。 - **输出检测**：U1800的输出检测通过VOSNS和GOSNS引脚实现。其中VOSNS连接到VCC0V75网络，GOSNS连接到GND。 - **使能控制**：U1800通过EN引脚进行硬件使能控制，EN引脚连接到N19888813网络。 - **I²C控制网络**：U1800的I²C控制通过SDA和SCL引脚连接，SDA连接到MCU_SDA_P15.5网络，SCL连接到MCU_SCL_P15.4网络。其I²C目标地址由VSEL引脚在上电时的状态决定，根据器件型号TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1，其默认I2C地址为0x42。 ### 详细依据 #### 输入信号 - **证据来源**：`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`，来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 - **证据内容**：该引脚映射显示，U1800的VIN引脚网络名为VCC3V3，这表明VCC3V3是U1800的电源输入。其他输入相关引脚如EN、MODE/SYNC、VSEL、FSEL的网络连接也一并列出，但它们属于控制或配置功能，并非主电源输入。 #### 输出检测 - **证据来源**：`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`，来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 - **证据内容**：该引脚映射显示，U1800的输出电压检测正端VOSNS连接到VCC0V75网络，检测负端GOSNS连接到GND。补偿管脚COMP连接至N19809013网络。 #### 使能控制 - **证据来源**：`circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800`，来自电路设计文件 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`。 - **证据内容**：该引脚映射显示，U1800的使能引脚EN连接至N19888813网络，表明U1800通过此网络上的信号进行硬件使能控制。 #### I²C控制网络 - **证据来源**：[1] `circuit:21:pin_mapping:U1800` (来自 `825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`)，[2] `document:4e4a4edc9a902323` (来自 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 的 Table 6-1)。 - **证据内容**： - 电路设计证据（来源1）表明，U1800的I²C数据线SDA连接到MCU_SDA_P15.5，时钟线SCL连接到MCU_SCL_P15.4。 - 器件手册证据（来源2）中的Table 6-1列出，对于型号为TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1的器件，其I²C地址为0x42。该表格的注释指出，I²C地址与启动电压相关，而启动电压由VSEL引脚的选择决定。结合电路设计中VSEL连接到N19808994，表明地址是由硬件配置的。 ### 缺失信息 根据现有证据，所有子问题的信息均已覆盖，未有明确缺失项。

voltage, and the output of the comparator goes high if the output voltage is either less than 95% (typical) or greater than 105% (typical) of the nominal output voltage. The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | <table><caption> Table 9-15. In a stacked configuration, in which all devices share a common enable signal, a thermal shutdown condition in one device disables the entire stack. When the hot device cools down, the whole stack automatically starts switching again. 9.3.17 Stacked Operation The user can connect multiple devices in parallel in what is known as a "stack"; for example, to increase output current capability or reduce device junction temperature. A stack comprises one primary device and one or more secondary devices. During initialization, each device monitors its SYNC_OUT pin to determine if must operate as a primary device or a secondary device: · If there is a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground, the device operates as a secondary device. · If the SYNC_OUT pin is high impedance, the device operates as a primary device. Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 The key points to note are: All the devices in the stack share a common enable signal, which must be pulled up with a resistance of at least 15 kQ. All the devices in the stack share a common power-good signal. All the devices in the stack share a common compensation signal. All secondary devices must connect a 47-kQ resistor between the SYNC_OUT pin and ground. The remote sense pins (VOSNS and GOSNS) of each device must be connected (do not leave these pins floating). Each device must be configured for the same switching frequency.· The primary device must be configured for forced PwM operation (secondary devices are automatically configured for forcedPwM operation). A stacked configuration can support synchronization to an external clock or spread-spectrum clocking.· Only the VSEL pin of the primary device is used to set the default output voltage. The VSEL pin of secondary· devices is not used and must be connected to ground. The SDA and SCL pins of secondary devices are not used and must be connected to ground. | <table><caption> Table 9-14. The switching frequency can be set to 1.5 MHz, 2.25 MHz, 2.5 MHz, or 3.0 MHz, or synchronized to an external clock in the same frequency range. The I2C-compatible interface offers several control, monitoring, and warning features, such as voltage monitoring and temperature-related warnings. The output voltage can be quickly adjusted to adapt the power consumption of the load to the performance needs of the application through the I2C-compatible interface. The default start-up voltage is resistor-selectable through the VSEL pin. 6 Device Options The I2C address is linked to the selected start-up voltage. The user cannot select the start-up voltage and I2C address independently.(1) The user can use the VSEL pin to select which of the four start-up voltages the device uses. Absolute Maximum Ratings do not imply functional operation of the device at these or any other conditions beyond those listed under Recommended Operating Conditions. If used outside the Recommended Operating Conditions but within the Absolute Maximum Ratings, the device may not be fully functional, and this may affect device reliability, functionality, performance, and shorten the device lifetime. (2)All voltage values are with respect to the GND pin. While switching. (3) | 10.4PowerSupplyRecommendations The TPS6287x-Q1 family has no special requirements for its input power supply. The output current rating of the input power supply must be rated according to the supply voltage and current requirements of the TPS6287X-Q1 10.5Layout 10.5.1 Layout Guidelines Achieving the performance the TPS6287x-Q1 devices are capable of requires proper PDN and PCB design. TI therefore recommends the user perform a power integrity analysis on their design. There are a number of commercially available power integrity software tools, and the user can use these tools to model the effects on performance of the PCB layout and passive components. In addition to the use of power integrity tools, Tl recommends the following basic principles: · Place the input capacitors close to the VIN and GND pins. Position the input capacitors in order of increasing size, starting with the smallest capacitors closest to the VIN and GND pins. Use an identical layout for both VIN-GND pin pairs of the package, to gain maximum benefit from the butterfly configuration. ·Place the inductor close to the device and keep the SW node small. · Connect the exposed thermal pad and the GND pins of the device together. Use multiple thermal vias to connect the exposed thermal pad of the device to one or more ground planes (Tl's EVM uses nine 150-μm thermal vias). ·Use multiple power and ground planes. · Route the VOSNS and GOSNS remote sense lines as a differential pair and connect them to the lowest- impedance point of the PDN. If the desired connection point is not the lowest impedance point of the PDN, optimize the PDN until it is. Do not route the VOSNS and GOSNS close to any of the switch nodes. · Connect the compensation components between VOSNS and GOSNS. Do not connect the compensation components directly to power ground. · If possible, distribute the output capacitors evenly between the TPS6287x-Q1 device and the point-of-load, rather than placing them altogether in one place. · Use multiple vias to connect each capacitor pad to the power and ground planes (TI's EVM typically uses four vias per pad). Use plenty of stitching vias to ensure a low impedance connection between different power and ground· planes. Note that the VSEL pin also sets the default start-up voltage of the device (see Table 9-4). TI recommends that the I?C controller initiates a STOP condition on the I2C bus after the initial power up of SDA and SCL pullup voltages to ensure reset of the I?C engine. 9.5.2 Standard, Fast, Fast Mode Plus Protocol The controller initiates a data transfer by generating a start condition. The start condition is when a high-to-low transition occurs on the SDA line while SCL is high, as shown in Figure 9-15. All I?C-compatible devices must recognize a start condition. The controller then generates the SCL pulses, and transmits the 7-bit address and the read and write direction bit R/W on the SDA line. During all transmissions, the controller makes sure that data is valid. A valid data condition requires the SDA line to be stable during the entire high period of the clock pulse (see Figure 9-16). All devices recognize the address sent by the controller and compare the address to their internal fixed addresses. Only the target with a matching address generates an acknowledge (see Figure 9-17) by puling the SDA line low during the entire high period of the ninth SCL cycle. Upon detecting this acknowledge, the controller knows that a communication link with a target has been established. The controller generates further SCL cycles to either transmit data to the target (R/W bit O) or receive data from the target (R/W bit 1). In either case, the target must acknowledge the data sent by the controller. So an acknowledge signal can either be generated by the controller or by the target, depending on which one is the receiver. 9-bit valid data sequences consisting of 8-bit data and 1-bit acknowledge can continue as long as necessary. To signal the end of the data transfer, the controller generates a stop condition by pulling the SDA line from low to high while the SCL line is high (see Figure 9-15). This stop condition releases the bus and stops the communication link with the addressed target. All I2C-compatible devices must recognize the stop condition. Upon the receipt of a stop condition, all devices know that the bus is released, and they wait for a start condition followed by a matching address. | If the user applies a valid clock signal to the MODE/SYNC pin, the device synchronizes its switching cycles to the external clock and automatically selects forced PwM operation. The MODE/SYNC pin is logically ORed with the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register (see Table 9-1). When multiple devices are used together in a stacked configuration, the MODE/SYNC pin of the secondary devices is the input for the clock signal (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.9 Spread Spectrum Clocking (SSC) The device has a spread spectrum clocking function that can reduce electromagnetic interference (EMl). When the SSC function is active, the device modulates the switching frequency to approximately ±10% the nominal value. The frequency modulation has a triangular characteristic (see Figure 9-11). To use the SSC function, make sure that: · SSCEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. · Forced PWM operation is selected (MODE pin is high or FPWMEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register). Product Folder Links: TPS62870-Q1TPS62871-Q1 TPS62872-Q1 TPS62873-Q1 ·The device is not synchronized to an external clock. To disable the SSC function, ensure that SCCEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. To use the SSC function with multiple devices in a stacked configuration, ensure that the primary converter runs from its internal oscillator and synchronize all secondary converters to the primary clock (see Figure 9-14). 9.3.10 Output Discharge The device has an output discharge function that ensures a defined ramp down of the output voltage when the device is disabled and keeps the output voltage close to 0 V while the device is off. The output discharge function is enabled when DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The output discharge function is enabled by default. If enabled, the device discharges the output under the following conditions: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = O in the CONTROL1 register. · A thermal shutdown event occurs. · A UVLO event occurs. · An OVLO event occurs. The output discharge function is not available until the user has enabled the device at least once after power up During power down, the device continues to discharge the output for as long as the internal supply voltage is greater than approximately 1.8 V. | all operating conditions. Page Changes from Revision A (July 2022) to Revision B (January 2023) | high level, LSB = 1. 9.3.6 Output Voltage Setting 9.3.6.1 Output Voltage Range The device has four different voltage ranges. The VRANGE[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register control which range is active (see Table 9-3). The default output voltage range after device initialization is 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps. Note that every change to the VRANGE[1:0] bits must be followed by a write to the VSET register, even if the value of the VSET[7:o] bits does not change. This sequence is required for the device to start to use the new voltage range. Also note that the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range uses a 0.8-V reference and the other ranges use a 0.4-V reference. Switching to and from the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range can therefore cause increased output voltage undershoot or overshoot while the device switches its internal reference. In device variants that do not have I?C, the output voltage range is factory-set to 0.4 V to 1.675 V. 9.3.6.2OutputVoltageSetpoint Together with the selected range, the VSET[7:0] bits in the VSET register control the output voltage setpoint of the device (see Table 9-4). During initialization, the device reads the state of the VSEL pin and selects the default output voltage according to Table 9-5. Note that the VSEL pin also selects the I?C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). If the user programs new output voltage setpoint (VOUT[7:0]), output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]), or soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings when the device has already begun its soft-start sequence, the device ignores the new values until the soft-start sequence is complete. If the user changes the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, ramps up or down to the new value. If the user changes VOUT[7:0], VRAMP[1:0], or SSTIME[1:0] while EN is low, the device uses the new values the next time the user enables it. | 10.3 Best Design Practices Figure 10-22.Shutdown (Discharge Enabled) 10.2.3 Application Curves (continued) 100MS/s 250MS/'s 3.30 3.30 V →3.0000ms T+600.000s Load = 0.75 Ω Load = 7.5 Q Figure 10-20. SWEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. The device junction temperature is below the thermal shutdown threshold.· · The supply voltage is below the OVLO threshold. All functions are available in on mode. The device leaves on mode and enters UVLO mode when the supply voltage decreases below the UVLO threshold. The device leaves on mode and enters standby mode when any of the following conditions is true: · A low level is applied to the EN pin. · SWEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. · The device junction temperature is greater than the thermal shutdown threshold. ·The supply voltage is greater than the OVLO threshold. 9.5 Programming 9.5.1 Serial Interface Description I2C is a 2-wire serial interface developed by Philips Semiconductor, now NXP Semiconductors (see I2C-Bus Specification and User Manual, Revision 6, 4 April 2014). The bus consists of a data line (SDA) and a clock line (SCL) with pullup structures. When the bus is idle, both SDA and SCL lines are pulled high. All I2C-compatible devices connect to the I2C bus through open-drain I/O pins, SDA and SCL. A controller, usually a microcontroller or a digital signal processor, controls the bus. The controller is responsible for generating the SCL signal and device addresses. The controller also generates specific conditions that indicate the START and STOP of data transfer. A target receives data, transmits data, or both on the bus under control of the controller. The TPS6287x-Q1 device operates as a target and supports the following data transfer modes, as defined in the I2C-Bus Specification: standard mode (100 kbps), fast mode (400 kbps), and fast mode plus (1 Mbps). The interface adds flexibility to the power supply solution, enabling most functions to be programmed to new values depending on the instantaneous application requirements. Register contents remain intact as long as the input voltage remains above 1.4 V. The data transfer protocol for standard and fast modes is exactly the same, therefore, they are referred to as F/S-mode in this document. The device supports 7-bit addressing; general call addresses are not supported. | <table><caption> Table 9-1 shows the function table of the MODE/SYNC pin and the FPWMEN bit in the CONTROL1 register, Table 9-1. FPWM Mode and Power-Save Mode Selection Note</caption> <tr><td >MODE/SYNCPin</td><td >FPWMEN Bit</td><td >OperatingMode</td><td >Remark</td></tr> <tr><td >Low</td><td >0</td><td >PSM</td><td >Do not use in a stacked configuration.</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >1</td><td >FPWM</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td >High</td><td >X</td><td >FPWM</td><td></td></tr> <tr><td >Sync Clock</td><td >X</td><td >FPWM</td><td></td></tr> </table> | Vin is the input voltage Vout is the output voltage.· · L is the effective inductance at the peak current level. tpd is the propagation delay of the current limit comparator (typically 50 ns). 9.3.13.2HiccupMode To enable hiccup operation, ensure that HICCUPEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | <table><caption> Table9-5.DefaultOutputVoltageSetpoints</caption> <tr><td >VSEL Pin(1)</td><td >Device Number</td><td >VSET[7:0]</td><td >Output Voltage Setpoint</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287x-Q1</td><td >0x5a</td><td >850 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY0-Q1</td><td >0x50</td><td >800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY1-Q1</td><td >0x14</td><td >500 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xY2-Q1</td><td >0x64</td><td >1800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td >6.2 kQ to GND</td><td >TPS6287xY3-Q1</td><td >0x52</td><td >810 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN0-Q1</td><td >0x14</td><td >500 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN1-Q1</td><td >0x50</td><td >800 mV</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td >TPS6287xN2-Q1</td><td >0x64</td><td >1800 mV</td></tr> </table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F11/F12/F13/F14/F15/F16/F17/F18/F19/F25/F27</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. 问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：根据现有证据，**可以部分回答**您的问题。文档声明了4个CAN相关模块，但网表只能部分确认其中1个模块（F23/CAN0）的连接细节，其余3个模块的连接信息在网表中缺失。 ### 1. 文档声明的CAN模块 文档明确声明了4个CAN相关模块： * **F20 (CAN3)**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯。[19][12] * **F21 (CAN2)**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯。[16][12] * **F22 (CAN1)**：基于TCAN1044A的CAN通讯。[17][12] * **F23 (CAN0)**：基于TCAN1044A（文档描述，支持兼容TCAN1145）的CAN通讯。[18][12] **支撑证据**：文档[12]中的模块列表明确列出了模块序号（F20-F23）、模块名称及功能定义。文档[16]-[19]（Port映射表）进一步定义了这四个模块的端口连接。 ### 2. 网表确认的CAN模块连接 网表**仅能确认F23 (CAN0)** 的部分连接。对于F20 (CAN3)、F21 (CAN2)、F22 (CAN1)，网表中**缺少**CAN2H/CAN2L、CAN3H/CAN3L等核心网络，无法确认其连接。 对于**F23 (CAN0)** 模块，网表可以确认以下连接： * **CAN总线差分信号**：网络 **CAN0H** 和 **CAN0L** 连接至连接器 `X1900`（具体引脚：CAN0H连X1900.2，CAN0L连X1900.3）。[1][2] * **收发器控制/状态信号**： * 网络 **CAN0_INH** 连接到收发器 `U1200.INH` 和 `U1203.INH`。[3] * 网络 **CAN0_INH_ACQ** 连接到 `U900A`（MCU）的 `AN20/EVADC_G2CH4/EDSADC_EDS2PA` 引脚。[4] * 网络 **CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8** 连接到收发器 `U1200.SCSN`、`U1202.STB`、`U1203.NSTB`。[5] * **CAN1模块**：网表仅确认了F22 (CAN1)的总线信号连接，缺少模块引脚连接。 * **CAN总线差分信号**：网络 **CAN1H** 和 **CAN1L** 连接至连接器 `X1900`（具体引脚：CAN1H连X1900.10，CAN1L连X1900.11）。[6][7] * 网表中未提供 `U1204`、`U1205` 等收发器的模块引脚连接（如TXD、RXD、STB等）信息。 **支撑证据**：网表电路设计数据[1]-[8]提供了上述网络的详细连接信息。 ### 3. 缺失信息 * **F20 (CAN3)模块**：网表中**缺失**CAN3H、CAN3L、CAN3_INH及与模块相关的收发器（未在网表中找到明确ID）的引脚连接信息。 * **F21 (CAN2)模块**：网表中**缺失**CAN2H、CAN2L及与模块相关的收发器（未在网表中找到明确ID）的引脚连接信息。虽然网表包含了F22 (CAN1)的CAN1H/L网络，但其收发器引脚连接（如 `U1204`、`U1205`）仍不完整。 * **模块编号与收发器芯片的对应关系**：文档中模块编号（F20-F23）与网表中收发器芯片（如U1200-U1205）的一一对应关系不明确，无法通过网表确认哪个收发器对应哪个F模块。 * **网表与文档的潜在冲突**：文档[18]明确声明F23 (CAN0)模块的收发器型号是TCAN1044A（兼容TCAN1145），但网表[5]显示该模块的控制信号 `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8` 同时连接了 `U1200.SCSN`（TCAN1145的SPI片选引脚）和 `U1203.NSTB`（TCAN1044A的待机引脚），表明实际设计中可能混合使用了不同类型的收发器，与文档的单一型号声明不完全一致。
